# Drawing F1.2 — Stepped Section Lines Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Ship the ANSI stepped-section view variant on top of F1 (full section) and F1.1 (half section) — `mode: 'stepped'` literal on `SectionView`, optional `stepSegments?: readonly StepSegment[]` field, sequential N-cut kernel pipeline reusing F1's `buildHalfSpace`, new `pickSteppedSection` tool with axis-locked polyline authoring, both renderer paths (live React canvas + SVG/PDF/PNG export) extended with `<polyline data-role="stepped-section-line">`.

**Architecture:** Extend the existing flat `SectionView` shape (F1.1's `'full' | 'half'` mode becomes `'full' | 'half' | 'stepped'`; no discriminated union — that refactor remains deferred per F1.1.1). The kernel grows a new `cutByStepSegments(oc, source, planes, arrowDirection)` export that runs N sequential `BRepAlgoAPI_Cut_3` calls — one per axis-aligned segment-derived half-space — reusing F1's `buildHalfSpace` unchanged. All cut faces tagged `planeSource: 'section'` uniformly; no `'step-jog'` provenance. The α-invariant has 7 clauses enforced at three layers (`deriveStepPlanes`, `AddSectionViewCommand.apply`, `projectSectionView` filter); each clause throws `'stepped-section-invariant-violated'` on breach. Authoring is a new `ToolMode = 'pickSteppedSection'` with axis-locked rubber band + Tab toggle + Shift-disable-snap; no live kernel preview during placement (SVG polyline overlay only); on Enter the empty-cut path surfaces a `'stepped-section-empty-cut'` toast. Both renderer paths (`SectionLineOverlay.tsx` live + `renderDrawingSvg.ts` export) are extended in the SAME batch per the F5 "two renderer paths" lesson. Manifest stays at v18 — `stepSegments?` is purely additive, no migration.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript, React, Zustand (`useToolModeStore`), Vitest, Playwright, Comlink + OCCT.js (kernel worker), OPFS (`sectionCache`).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-22-drawing-f1.2-stepped-section-design.md` (committed at `ccff4b4`, 886 lines, all 17 design axes locked).

**Baseline at plan start:** master HEAD `ccff4b4` (post-spec). Test totals to maintain green between batches: **2378 unit** (586 shared + 37 sketcher-worker + 335 kernel + 1420 app) + **13 drawing e2e** (T-6-1..T-6-12b) + **10 perf** — all green. Branch will be `feature/drawing-f1.2-stepped-section` created post-plan via the `superpowers:using-git-worktrees` skill.

## Batch 1: Foundation — `StepSegment` type, `SectionView` union extension, α-invariant validator, `deriveStepPlanes` helper, SS-MIG-1 (~7 tasks)

Batch 1 lays the pure-TypeScript foundation that every later batch depends on: the new `StepSegment`
type export, the `'stepped'` literal added to the `SectionView['mode']` union, the optional
`stepSegments?` field, the `validateSteppedInvariant` runtime guard with all 7 α-invariant clauses
built up red→green clause-by-clause, the `deriveStepPlanes` happy-path helper, and the SS-MIG-1
v18-stepped round-trip extension to the existing `migrateManifest-v17-to-v18.spec.ts`. No kernel,
command, projector, or UI code is touched. All changes live in `packages/shared`. Manifest stays at
v18 (the new fields are additive per spec §4.6 / §9.1; no version bump).

Baseline counts at batch start (master HEAD `8941382`): shared 503+ unit tests in scope (the
2378 + drawing-e2e + perf baseline is global). After Batch 1 the shared package should grow by
**~12 new unit tests** (1 type-shape + 7 α-clause sub-tests inside SS-SP-4 + 3 deriveStepPlanes
happy-path tests SS-SP-1..SS-SP-3 + 1 round-trip SS-MIG-1). Downstream packages (kernel, app) are
**not** typechecked in this batch's exit criteria — adding the `'stepped'` literal to the union
will not break any existing `view.mode === 'half'` / `=== 'full'` branch, and no exhaustive switch
exists in shared. Kernel/app cascade work lands in Batches 2–4.

```ts
// --- Stepped-section validator (Drawing F1.2) ---

import { validateSteppedInvariant } from '../sectionPlane';
import type { SectionView } from '../types';

type SteppedValidatorInput = Pick<SectionView, 'mode' | 'stepSegments' | 'cutSide' | 'splitPlane'>;

describe('SectionView — stepped mode + stepSegments shape (F1.2 type extension)', () => {
  it("SectionView accepts mode: 'stepped' with stepSegments and no cutSide/splitPlane", () => {
    const sv: SectionView = {
      id: 'sv-step',
      kind: 'section',
      parentViewId: 'v1',
      sectionLine: { p0: { x: 0, y: 0 }, p1: { x: 10, y: 10 } },
      arrowDirection: 1,
      label: 'A',
      positionMm: { x: 10, y: 130 },
      scale: { numerator: 1, denominator: 1 },
      showHidden: false,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: [
        { startSheet: { x: 0,  y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 10, y: 0  } },
        { startSheet: { x: 10, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 10, y: 10 } },
      ],
      hatchSegments: [],
      fingerprint: '',
    };
    expect(sv.mode).toBe('stepped');
    expect(sv.stepSegments).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(sv.cutSide).toBeUndefined();
    expect(sv.splitPlane).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('validateSteppedInvariant — α-invariant clauses (SS-SP-4)', () => {
  // SS-SP-4a — clause 1: stepSegments !== undefined
  it('SS-SP-4a: throws when mode === stepped but stepSegments is undefined', () => {
    const view: SteppedValidatorInput = {
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: undefined,
      cutSide: undefined,
      splitPlane: undefined,
    };
    expect(() => validateSteppedInvariant(view)).toThrow(/stepped-section-invariant-violated:.*stepSegments must be defined/);
  });

  it('does not throw when mode !== stepped (validator is a no-op for full/half)', () => {
    const fullView: SteppedValidatorInput = {
      mode: 'full',
      stepSegments: undefined,
      cutSide: undefined,
      splitPlane: undefined,
    };
    expect(() => validateSteppedInvariant(fullView)).not.toThrow();
    const halfView: SteppedValidatorInput = {
      mode: 'half',
      stepSegments: undefined,
      cutSide: 1,
      splitPlane: { origin: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, normal: { x: 1, y: 0, z: 0 } },
    };
    expect(() => validateSteppedInvariant(halfView)).not.toThrow();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement minimum code to pass**

**Change A — extend the `SectionView` type in `packages/shared/src/drawing/types.ts`.** Replace
the F1.1 block at lines 136–144 with the F1.2 block (adds `'stepped'` to the union and inserts
the new `stepSegments?` field):

```ts
  // --- NEW in F1.1 (Drawing Half-Section) ---
  readonly mode: 'full' | 'half' | 'stepped';   // 'stepped' is NEW in F1.2; no manifest bump (additive)
  readonly cutSide?: -1 | 1;        // present iff mode === 'half'
  readonly splitPlane?: {           // present iff mode === 'half'
    readonly origin: { readonly x: number; readonly y: number; readonly z: number };
    readonly normal: { readonly x: number; readonly y: number; readonly z: number };
  };
  // --- NEW in F1.2 (Drawing Stepped Section) ---
  readonly stepSegments?: readonly StepSegment[];  // present iff mode === 'stepped'
  // α-invariant: mode === 'half'    ⟺ cutSide !== undefined && splitPlane !== undefined
  // α-invariant: mode === 'stepped' ⟺ stepSegments !== undefined
  //                                && stepSegments.length in [2..8]
  //                                && every segment is axis-aligned (dx===0 XOR dy===0)
  //                                && consecutive segments share an endpoint
  //                                && jog directions alternate
  //                                && cutSide === undefined && splitPlane === undefined.
  // Enforced at three layers (validateSteppedInvariant in sectionPlane.ts, command boundary,
  // and projector filter). Throws 'stepped-section-invariant-violated' on breach.
```

**Change B — add `validateSteppedInvariant` to `packages/shared/src/drawing/sectionPlane.ts`.**
Update the existing import at line 10 to also pull `SectionView` + `StepSegment`:

```ts
import type { Vec2, SectionView, StepSegment } from './types';
```

Then append at the end of the file (after `sideOfSectionLine`):

```ts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// validateSteppedInvariant (Drawing F1.2 — Stepped section α-invariant guard)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Validate the α-invariant for a stepped SectionView. No-op when
 * `view.mode !== 'stepped'`. Throws `Error('stepped-section-invariant-violated: ...')`
 * with a clause-specific suffix on the first clause that fails.
 *
 * Clauses (built up red→green clause-by-clause through Batch 1, Tasks 1.2 → 1.7):
 *   1. stepSegments !== undefined            (this task, SS-SP-4a)
 *   2. stepSegments.length >= 2              (Task 1.3, SS-SP-4b)
 *   3. stepSegments.length <= 8              (Task 1.4, SS-SP-4c)
 *   4. every segment is axis-aligned         (Task 1.5, SS-SP-4d)
 *   5. consecutive segments share endpoints  (Task 1.6, SS-SP-4e)
 *   6. consecutive segments alternate axes   (Task 1.6, SS-SP-4f)
 *   7. cutSide and splitPlane undefined      (Task 1.7, SS-SP-4g)
 */
export function validateSteppedInvariant(
  view: Pick<SectionView, 'mode' | 'stepSegments' | 'cutSide' | 'splitPlane'>,
): void {
  if (view.mode !== 'stepped') return;

  // Clause 1 — stepSegments must be defined.
  if (view.stepSegments === undefined) {
    throw new Error('stepped-section-invariant-violated: stepSegments must be defined');
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run test to verify it fails for the right reason**

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement minimum code to pass**

Extend `validateSteppedInvariant` in `packages/shared/src/drawing/sectionPlane.ts` by adding the
clause-2 check **after** the clause-1 check:

```ts
  // Clause 1 — stepSegments must be defined.
  if (view.stepSegments === undefined) {
    throw new Error('stepped-section-invariant-violated: stepSegments must be defined');
  }
  // Clause 2 — stepSegments.length must be at least 2 (a single segment is a full section).
  if (view.stepSegments.length < 2) {
    throw new Error(
      'stepped-section-invariant-violated: stepSegments.length must be >= 2 (got ' +
        view.stepSegments.length + ')',
    );
  }
```

```ts
  // SS-SP-4c — clause 3: stepSegments.length <= 8

        { startSheet: { x:  0, y:  0 }, endSheet: { x: 10, y:  0 } }, // H
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement minimum code to pass**

Extend `validateSteppedInvariant` in `packages/shared/src/drawing/sectionPlane.ts` by adding the
clause-4 loop **after** the clause-3 check:

```ts
  // Clause 4 — every segment is axis-aligned: dx===0 XOR dy===0.
  // Tolerance 1e-9 absorbs float-arith noise; degenerate zero-length segments fall here too.
  for (let i = 0; i < view.stepSegments.length; i++) {
    const seg = view.stepSegments[i]!;
    const dx = seg.endSheet.x - seg.startSheet.x;
    const dy = seg.endSheet.y - seg.startSheet.y;
    const horiz = Math.abs(dy) < 1e-9 && Math.abs(dx) > 1e-9;
    const vert  = Math.abs(dx) < 1e-9 && Math.abs(dy) > 1e-9;
    if (!horiz && !vert) {
      throw new Error(
        'stepped-section-invariant-violated: segments must be axis-aligned ' +
          '(segment ' + i + ' has dx=' + dx + ', dy=' + dy + ')',
      );
    }
  }
```

```ts
  // Clause 5 — consecutive segments share an endpoint.
  // segments[i].endSheet must equal segments[i+1].startSheet (within 1e-9 mm).
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < view.stepSegments.length; i++) {
    const a = view.stepSegments[i]!;
    const b = view.stepSegments[i + 1]!;
    const dx = a.endSheet.x - b.startSheet.x;
    const dy = a.endSheet.y - b.startSheet.y;
    if (Math.abs(dx) > 1e-9 || Math.abs(dy) > 1e-9) {
      throw new Error(
        'stepped-section-invariant-violated: consecutive segments must share an endpoint ' +
          '(segment ' + i + '.endSheet (' + a.endSheet.x + ',' + a.endSheet.y +
          ') != segment ' + (i + 1) + '.startSheet (' + b.startSheet.x + ',' + b.startSheet.y + '))',
      );
    }
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 3b: Implement clause-6 check**

Extend `validateSteppedInvariant` in `packages/shared/src/drawing/sectionPlane.ts` by adding the

function (closure scope) so it captures the same tolerance convention:

```ts
  // Clause 6 — consecutive segments alternate axes (H-V-H-V or V-H-V-H).
  // axisOf returns 'x' if the segment is horizontal (dy ≈ 0), else 'y'. Pre-clause-4
  // pass guarantees one of dx/dy is ≈ 0 and the other is not — so axisOf is well-defined.
  const axisOf = (seg: StepSegment): 'x' | 'y' =>
    Math.abs(seg.endSheet.y - seg.startSheet.y) < 1e-9 ? 'x' : 'y';
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < view.stepSegments.length; i++) {
    const axA = axisOf(view.stepSegments[i]!);
    const axB = axisOf(view.stepSegments[i + 1]!);
    if (axA === axB) {
      throw new Error(
        'stepped-section-invariant-violated: consecutive segments must alternate axes ' +
          '(segment ' + i + ' and segment ' + (i + 1) + ' are both on the ' + axA + ' axis)',
      );
    }
  }
```

#### Sub-slice 7b–7d — `deriveStepPlanes` happy path (SS-SP-1, SS-SP-2, SS-SP-3)

- [ ] **Step 1b: Write the failing tests for SS-SP-1, SS-SP-2, SS-SP-3**

Append the following new `describe` block at the end of
`packages/shared/src/drawing/__tests__/sectionPlane.spec.ts` (after the closing `});` of the
`'validateSteppedInvariant — α-invariant clauses (SS-SP-4)'` describe):

```ts
// --- deriveStepPlanes happy-path (SS-SP-1 .. SS-SP-3) ---

import { deriveStepPlanes } from '../sectionPlane';

describe('deriveStepPlanes (SS-SP-1..SS-SP-3)', () => {
  // SS-SP-1 — Front view, 2 axis-aligned segments, arrowDirection +1
  //   seg0 horizontal (0,0)→(60,0): lineDir=(1,0,0) view; splitNormal2D=(0,1,0); world ≈ (0,1,0).
  //   seg1 vertical   (60,0)→(60,30): lineDir=(0,1,0) view; splitNormal2D=(-1,0,0); world ≈ (-1,0,0).
  it('SS-SP-1: front view, 2 axis-aligned segments → 2 orthogonal planes with normals along world-Y and world-X', () => {
    const planes = deriveStepPlanes(
      [
        { startSheet: { x:  0, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 60, y:  0 } }, // H
        { startSheet: { x: 60, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 60, y: 30 } }, // V
      ],
      FRONT_VIEW_MATRIX,
      1,
    );
    expect(planes).toHaveLength(2);

    // plane 0: normal along +Y in world
    expect(approx(normLen(planes[0]!.normal), 1.0)).toBe(true);
    expect(approx(planes[0]!.normal.x, 0, 1e-4)).toBe(true);
    expect(planes[0]!.normal.y).toBeGreaterThan(0.9);
    expect(approx(planes[0]!.normal.z, 0, 1e-4)).toBe(true);

    // plane 1: normal along -X in world
    expect(approx(normLen(planes[1]!.normal), 1.0)).toBe(true);
    expect(planes[1]!.normal.x).toBeLessThan(-0.9);
    expect(approx(planes[1]!.normal.y, 0, 1e-4)).toBe(true);
    expect(approx(planes[1]!.normal.z, 0, 1e-4)).toBe(true);

    // Orthogonality cross-check: dot(plane0.normal, plane1.normal) ≈ 0
    const dot = planes[0]!.normal.x * planes[1]!.normal.x
              + planes[0]!.normal.y * planes[1]!.normal.y
              + planes[0]!.normal.z * planes[1]!.normal.z;
    expect(approx(dot, 0, 1e-6)).toBe(true);
  });

  // SS-SP-2 — Same geometry, arrowDirection -1 → all normals negated relative to SS-SP-1.
  it('SS-SP-2: arrowDirection -1 → all planes’ normals flipped', () => {
    const planesPos = deriveStepPlanes(
      [
        { startSheet: { x:  0, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 60, y:  0 } },
        { startSheet: { x: 60, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 60, y: 30 } },
      ],
      FRONT_VIEW_MATRIX,
      1,
    );
    const planesNeg = deriveStepPlanes(
      [
        { startSheet: { x:  0, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 60, y:  0 } },
        { startSheet: { x: 60, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 60, y: 30 } },
      ],
      FRONT_VIEW_MATRIX,
      -1,
    );
    expect(planesNeg).toHaveLength(2);

    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      const np = planesPos[i]!.normal;
      const nn = planesNeg[i]!.normal;
      expect(approx(nn.x, -np.x, 1e-6)).toBe(true);
      expect(approx(nn.y, -np.y, 1e-6)).toBe(true);
      expect(approx(nn.z, -np.z, 1e-6)).toBe(true);
      // Origins are identical (same segment midpoints; not affected by arrowDirection).
      expect(approx(planesNeg[i]!.origin.x, planesPos[i]!.origin.x, 1e-6)).toBe(true);
      expect(approx(planesNeg[i]!.origin.y, planesPos[i]!.origin.y, 1e-6)).toBe(true);
      expect(approx(planesNeg[i]!.origin.z, planesPos[i]!.origin.z, 1e-6)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  // SS-SP-3 — Top view, 2 axis-aligned segments → planes correctly transformed into world space.
  // Structural check: unit-length normals, perpendicular to each other.
  it('SS-SP-3: top view, 2 axis-aligned segments → planes transformed into world space (structural check)', () => {
    const planes = deriveStepPlanes(
      [
        { startSheet: { x:  0, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 50, y:  0 } }, // H in top view
        { startSheet: { x: 50, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 50, y: 40 } }, // V in top view
      ],
      TOP_VIEW_MATRIX,
      1,
    );
    expect(planes).toHaveLength(2);

    // Both normals unit-length
    expect(approx(normLen(planes[0]!.normal), 1.0)).toBe(true);
    expect(approx(normLen(planes[1]!.normal), 1.0)).toBe(true);

    // Top-view sees XZ plane: view-Y on screen = world -Z. Plane normals therefore live in the
    // world XZ subspace (i.e., world Y component ≈ 0).
    expect(approx(planes[0]!.normal.y, 0, 1e-4)).toBe(true);
    expect(approx(planes[1]!.normal.y, 0, 1e-4)).toBe(true);

    // Orthogonality of normals
    const dot = planes[0]!.normal.x * planes[1]!.normal.x
              + planes[0]!.normal.y * planes[1]!.normal.y
              + planes[0]!.normal.z * planes[1]!.normal.z;
    expect(approx(dot, 0, 1e-6)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3b: Implement `deriveStepPlanes`**

Append the following export to `packages/shared/src/drawing/sectionPlane.ts` **after**
`validateSteppedInvariant` (i.e., at the very end of the file):

```ts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// deriveStepPlanes (Drawing F1.2 — Stepped section view)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Derive one world-space `Plane` per segment of a stepped section view. Each
 * segment's plane is computed by the same formula as `deriveSplitPlane`:
 * the plane contains the segment's midpoint in world space, with its normal
 * = (perpendicular-to-segment in view XY) mapped through the parent view's
 * inverse matrix. `arrowDirection` plays the same role as `cutSide` does for
 * half-section: it selects which side of each plane is the "keep" side.
 *
 * Validates the α-invariant first (clauses 1–7); throws
 * `Error('stepped-section-invariant-violated: ...')` on breach.
 *
 * @param stepSegments      - polyline of axis-aligned segments in parent-view-local mm
 * @param parentViewMatrix  - row-major 4×4 view-to-world matrix of the parent view
 * @param arrowDirection    - which side of each plane to remove (-1 or +1)
 * @returns N planes, one per segment, in input order.
 */
export function deriveStepPlanes(
  stepSegments: readonly StepSegment[],
  parentViewMatrix: Mat4,
  arrowDirection: -1 | 1,
): Plane[] {
  validateSteppedInvariant({
    mode: 'stepped',
    stepSegments,
    cutSide: undefined,
    splitPlane: undefined,
  });

  return stepSegments.map(seg =>
    deriveSplitPlane(
      { p0: seg.startSheet, p1: seg.endSheet },
      parentViewMatrix,
      arrowDirection,
    ),
  );
}
```

## Batch 2: Kernel `cutByStepSegments` + assembly variant + app-side wrapper (~7 tasks)

Batch 2 is the kernel core of the stepped-section feature. With Batch 1's `StepSegment` type, the
extended `SectionView` shape, and the `deriveStepPlanes` helper already in place, this batch
introduces the two new kernel exports that turn an array of world-space planes into a sequentially
cut compound with correctly tagged faces: `cutByStepSegments` (single source) and
`cutAssemblyByStepSegments` (assembly mirror). Every cut face produced by every segment is
uniformly tagged `planeSource: 'section'` per spec §5 face-tag contract — F1.1's `'split'` value
is never emitted by this code path. The implementation reuses `buildHalfSpace()` from F1
unchanged and runs N sequential `BRepAlgoAPI_Cut_3` calls.

All work lives in two files: `packages/kernel/src/section/cutBySectionPlane.ts` (extended with two
new exports) and `packages/kernel/src/section/cutBySectionPlane.spec.ts` (extended with SS-K-1..10).
Baseline kernel-unit-test count at Batch 2 start: existing post-F1.1 count. Expected delta after
Batch 2 completes: **+10 unit tests** (SS-K-1 through SS-K-10). Full-section and half-section
paths are unchanged — Batch 2 is purely additive to the kernel module.

- [ ] **Step 2: Pin §5.2 pseudocode at the top of `cutByStepSegments` as a TSDoc reference**

The full spec §5.2 pseudocode (verbatim) — this is the contract `cutByStepSegments` must
implement across Tasks 2.1–2.5. Quote this as a code-block comment immediately above the function
in the implementation file so future readers can map the body to the spec line-by-line:

```text
function cutByStepSegments(oc, source, planes, arrowDirection):
    if planes.length < 2: throw 'stepped-section-invariant-violated' (count < 2)
    if planes.length > 8: throw 'stepped-section-invariant-violated' (count > 8)

    running = source
    mergedTags = empty FaceTagMap
    anyCut = false

    for i in 0 .. planes.length - 1:
        plane = planes[i]
        halfSpace = buildHalfSpace(oc, plane, arrowDirection)
        result = BRepAlgoAPI_Cut_3(running, halfSpace.handle)
        if !result.IsDone() or no solid in result:
            // Whole stepped cut is empty: signal degraded state
            return { ok: false, reason: 'empty' }

        next = result.Shape()

        // Volume tangent check on per-segment basis
        if abs(volume(next) - volume(running)) < 1e-6 * volume(running):
            // This segment's plane was tangent; preserve running compound, skip merge
            // (Per-segment tangent is non-fatal — a stepped cut with a tangent segment
            //  still produces valid geometry from the other segments.)
            release(next)
            release(halfSpace)
            continue

        // Walk faces of `next`; tag any face within 1e-4 mm of `plane` as
        // cut-face with planeSource: 'section'. All segments share planeSource.
        for face in faces(next):
            key = hashFace(face)
            if classifyFace(face, plane) === 'cut-face':
                mergedTags.set(key, {
                    tag: 'cut-face',
                    partId: '', materialId: '',
                    planeSource: 'section',
                    polygon3D: extractFaceBoundary3D(face),
                })
                continue
            // Preserve prior cut-face tags from earlier segments (don't overwrite as 'original'):
            if mergedTags.has(key):
                continue
            mergedTags.set(key, { tag: 'original-face', partId: '', materialId: '' })

        release(running) unless i === 0   // never delete the caller's source
        running = next
        release(halfSpace)
        anyCut = true

    if !anyCut:
        return { ok: false, reason: 'empty' }   // every segment was tangent

    return { ok: true, compound: running, faceTags: mergedTags }
```

**Face-tag contract (spec §5 / B2 axis):** every cut face from every segment carries
`planeSource: 'section'` uniformly. Do **NOT** introduce a `'step-jog'` value. Do **NOT**
overwrite a prior segment's `'cut-face'` tag with `'original-face'` when a later segment classifies
the same face as non-cut.

```ts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// cutByStepSegments (Drawing F1.2 — Stepped-section views)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
//
// Spec §5.2 reference pseudocode — see docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-22-drawing-f1.2-stepped-section-design.md
//
// function cutByStepSegments(oc, source, planes, arrowDirection):
//     if planes.length < 2: throw 'stepped-section-invariant-violated' (count < 2)
//     if planes.length > 8: throw 'stepped-section-invariant-violated' (count > 8)
//     ... (running compound, per-segment cut, per-segment tangent skip, face tagging) ...

/**
 * Cut `source` compound by a polyline of N axis-aligned half-spaces, one per
 * segment of an ANSI stepped section line. Runs N sequential BRepAlgoAPI_Cut_3
 * calls, reusing F1's `buildHalfSpace` per segment. All faces classified as
 * cut-faces by any segment carry `planeSource: 'section'`. See spec §5 for
 * the full face-tag contract.
 *
 * Throws 'stepped-section-invariant-violated' when planes.length < 2 or > 8.
 * Returns { ok: false, reason: 'empty' } when the cumulative cut produces no
 * solid or every segment is tangent.
 */
export function cutByStepSegments(
  oc: OcctInstance,
  source: unknown /* TopoDS_Compound */,
  planes: readonly Plane[],
  arrowDirection: -1 | 1,
): Result {
  if (planes.length < 2) {
    throw new Error(`stepped-section-invariant-violated: planes.length=${planes.length} < 2 (clause 2)`);
  }
  // Tasks 2.2–2.5 will extend this body with the count-> 8 guard, the main
  // BRepAlgoAPI_Cut_3 loop, the per-segment volume-tangent skip, the face
  // classifier, and the empty-cut handler.
  throw new Error('cutByStepSegments: loop body not yet implemented (Task 2.2)');
}
```

The placeholder `throw new Error('... not yet implemented (Task 2.2)')` ensures any premature
multi-segment call surfaces loudly. Task 2.2's SS-K-5 + SS-K-1 will replace this with the real
loop.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing test — SS-K-1 (canonical 2-segment cut)**

The fixture is a 100×60×40 box at the OCCT origin (so x∈[0,100], y∈[0,60], z∈[0,40]). The two
planes form a single jog: cut from above at z=20 (plane 1, normal +Z), then cut from the right
at x=50 (plane 2, normal +X). With `arrowDirection=1`, `buildHalfSpace` removes the side opposite
the normal — that is, the half-space pointing along `-normal * arrowDirection`. For plane 1 (+Z,
arrow +1), `buildHalfSpace` removes the +Z half-space (z > 20 is kept by the cut, so result
keeps z ≤ 20). For plane 2 (+X, arrow +1), result keeps x ≤ 50.

Result: a smaller box, x∈[0,50], y∈[0,60], z∈[0,20], volume = 60 000 mm³.

Append:

```ts
it('SS-K-1: canonical 2-segment cut on 100×60×40 box — both planes contribute, all faces tagged "section"', () => {
  const box = makeBox(oc, 100, 60, 40);
  const compound = buildTestCompound(oc, box);
  const planes: Plane[] = [
    { origin: { x: 0,  y: 0, z: 20 }, normal: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 1 } }, // segment 1: horizontal cut
    { origin: { x: 50, y: 0, z: 0  }, normal: { x: 1, y: 0, z: 0 } }, // segment 2: vertical cut (one jog)
  ];
  const result = cutByStepSegments(oc, compound, planes, 1);
  expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
  if (!result.ok) return;

  const tags = [...result.faceTags.values()];
  const cutFaces = tags.filter(t => t.tag === 'cut-face');
  // Each segment contributes at least one cut face on a box; total >= 2.
  expect(cutFaces.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);

  // Face-tag contract: every cut face from any segment is 'section' (B2 axis).
  // The 'split' value from F1.1 must NEVER appear in stepped cuts.
  for (const face of cutFaces) {
    expect((face as { planeSource?: string }).planeSource).toBe('section');
  }
  const splitFaces = tags.filter(t => (t as { planeSource?: string }).planeSource === 'split');
  expect(splitFaces).toHaveLength(0);

  disposeOcctShape(oc, result.compound);
  (box.handle as { delete(): void }).delete();
});
```

Run:

```bash
pnpm --filter @zilventor/kernel test:unit -- cutBySectionPlane.spec.ts -t "SS-K-1"
```

Expected: FAIL — `Error: cutByStepSegments: loop body not yet implemented (Task 2.2)` (the
placeholder throw from Task 2.1).

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement minimal loop body**

Replace the placeholder `throw` in `cutByStepSegments` with the loop below. This minimal
implementation handles the happy 2-segment path: no per-segment tangent skip yet (Task 2.5), no
preserve-prior-cut-face merge (Task 2.3), no empty-cut guard (Task 2.4). Those slices are layered
in by later tasks; their tests will surface the gaps.

```ts
export function cutByStepSegments(
  oc: OcctInstance,
  source: unknown /* TopoDS_Compound */,
  planes: readonly Plane[],
  arrowDirection: -1 | 1,
): Result {
  if (planes.length < 2) {
    throw new Error(`stepped-section-invariant-violated: planes.length=${planes.length} < 2 (clause 2)`);
  }
  if (planes.length > 8) {
    throw new Error(`stepped-section-invariant-violated: planes.length=${planes.length} > 8 (clause 3)`);
  }

  let running: unknown = source;
  const mergedTags: FaceTagMap = new Map();

  for (let i = 0; i < planes.length; i += 1) {
    const plane = planes[i]!;
    const halfSpace = buildHalfSpace(oc, plane, arrowDirection);

    const rA = new oc.Message_ProgressRange_1();
    const rB = new oc.Message_ProgressRange_1();
    let next: unknown;
    let ok = false;
    try {
      const cutter = new oc.BRepAlgoAPI_Cut_3(running as never, halfSpace.handle as never, rA);
      cutter.Build(rB);
      if (cutter.IsDone()) {
        next = cutter.Shape();
        ok = true;
      }
      cutter.delete();
    } finally {
      rA.delete();
      rB.delete();
      (halfSpace.handle as unknown as { delete(): void }).delete();
    }
    if (!ok) {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'empty' };
    }

    // Walk faces of `next`; classify against this segment's plane.
    const faceExp = new oc.TopExp_Explorer_2(
      next as never,
      oc.TopAbs_ShapeEnum.TopAbs_FACE as never,
      oc.TopAbs_ShapeEnum.TopAbs_SHAPE as never,
    );
    while (faceExp.More()) {
      const face = faceExp.Current();
      const key = hashFace(oc, face);
      if (classifyFace(oc, face, plane) === 'cut-face') {
        const polygon3D = extractFaceBoundary3D(oc, face);
        mergedTags.set(key, {
          tag: 'cut-face',
          partId: '',
          materialId: '',
          planeSource: 'section',
          ...(polygon3D.length ? { polygon3D } : {}),
        });
      } else {
        // NOTE: this naive set may overwrite a prior segment's cut-face tag —
        // Task 2.3 adds the preserve-prior-cut-face guard.
        mergedTags.set(key, { tag: 'original-face', partId: '', materialId: '' });
      }
      faceExp.Next();
    }
    faceExp.delete();

    if (i > 0) {
      (running as unknown as { delete?(): void }).delete?.();
    }
    running = next;
  }

  return { ok: true, compound: running, faceTags: mergedTags };
}
```

### Task 2.3: SS-K-2 (4-segment volume reduction + preserve prior cut-face tags)

**Spec section:** §5.2 (mergedTags accumulator), §5.3 (face-tag contract), §10.1 (SS-K-2)
**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/kernel/src/section/cutBySectionPlane.ts` (add preserve-prior-cut-face guard in the face loop)
- Modify: `packages/kernel/src/section/cutBySectionPlane.spec.ts` (append SS-K-2)

**Red→green→refactor slice:** With a 4-segment plane sequence (3 jogs) on the same 100×60×40 box,
`cutByStepSegments` returns `{ok:true}` with the result compound's volume strictly less than every
intermediate volume (monotonic reduction). Every cut face produced anywhere along the sequence
retains its `planeSource: 'section'` tag — later segments must NOT downgrade an earlier
segment's cut face to `'original-face'`.

The behavioural delta vs Task 2.2's body: today (after 2.2) the face loop unconditionally
overwrites the `mergedTags` entry for any non-cut face. That means a face that was a `cut-face`
under segment 1 but a non-cut under segment 2 gets reset to `original-face`. SS-K-2 surfaces this
bug; the fix is the §5.2 "preserve prior cut-face tags" guard.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test — SS-K-2**

Append inside the SS-K-1..10 describe block:

```ts
it('SS-K-2: 4-segment / 3-jog cut on 100×60×40 box — monotonic volume reduction, prior cut-face tags preserved', () => {
  const box = makeBox(oc, 100, 60, 40);
  const compound = buildTestCompound(oc, box);

  // 4 axis-aligned segments forming 3 jogs:
  //  z=30 (top) → x=70 (right) → z=10 (bottom step) → x=30 (left).
  // Each plane carves a different region; the kept compound shrinks at every step.
  const planes: Plane[] = [
    { origin: { x: 0,  y: 0, z: 30 }, normal: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 1 } },
    { origin: { x: 70, y: 0, z: 0  }, normal: { x: 1, y: 0, z: 0 } },
    { origin: { x: 0,  y: 0, z: 10 }, normal: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 1 } },
    { origin: { x: 30, y: 0, z: 0  }, normal: { x: 1, y: 0, z: 0 } },
  ];

  const baselineVol = shapeVolumePublic(oc, compound);
  const result = cutByStepSegments(oc, compound, planes, 1);
  expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
  if (!result.ok) return;

  // Monotonic reduction: final volume strictly less than baseline.
  const finalVol = shapeVolumePublic(oc, result.compound);
  expect(finalVol).toBeLessThan(baselineVol);
  expect(finalVol).toBeGreaterThan(0); // not over-cut into empty

  // Face-tag contract: every cut face — accumulated across all 4 segments —
  // must carry planeSource:'section'. None should have been clobbered back to
  // 'original-face' by a later segment.
  const tags = [...result.faceTags.values()];
  const cutFaces = tags.filter(t => t.tag === 'cut-face');
  expect(cutFaces.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2); // at least two distinct cut faces survive
  for (const face of cutFaces) {
    expect((face as { planeSource?: string }).planeSource).toBe('section');
  }

  disposeOcctShape(oc, result.compound);
  (box.handle as { delete(): void }).delete();
});
```

- FAIL with at least one cut face carrying `planeSource: undefined` (because Task 2.2's naive

If by accident the 4-segment fixture does not surface the regression (faces from different

  .toHaveLength(0)` and adjust the geometry so at least one face is hit by two segments. Do not
proceed until the test surfaces the bug.

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the preserve-prior-cut-face guard**

In `cutByStepSegments`, replace the face-classification `else` branch so it no longer overwrites
an existing `cut-face` entry. The minimal edit is two extra lines inside the face loop:

```ts
      if (classifyFace(oc, face, plane) === 'cut-face') {
        const polygon3D = extractFaceBoundary3D(oc, face);
        mergedTags.set(key, {
          tag: 'cut-face',
          partId: '',
          materialId: '',
          planeSource: 'section',
          ...(polygon3D.length ? { polygon3D } : {}),
        });
      } else {
        // Preserve prior segment's cut-face tag — never downgrade to 'original-face'.
        // Per spec §5.2: `if mergedTags.has(key): continue`.
        if (mergedTags.has(key)) {
          // already classified by an earlier segment (as cut-face) — leave it.
        } else {
          mergedTags.set(key, { tag: 'original-face', partId: '', materialId: '' });
        }
      }
```

### Task 2.5: SS-K-10 (per-segment tangent skip)

**Spec section:** §5.2 (volume-tangent check), §5.4 (per-segment tangent non-fatal), §10.1 (SS-K-10)
**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/kernel/src/section/cutBySectionPlane.ts` (add per-segment volume-tangent skip + console.warn)
- Modify: `packages/kernel/src/section/cutBySectionPlane.spec.ts` (append SS-K-10)

**Red→green→refactor slice:** A 2-segment cut where segment 1 is tangent (its plane is
coincident with an existing face on the source — volume unchanged) and segment 2 is a valid cut.
Expected behaviour:
- `result.ok === true` (per-segment tangent is non-fatal).
- The valid segment's cut faces are tagged `planeSource: 'section'`.
- `console.warn` is called with a message mentioning `tangent segment`.
- The tangent segment contributes **no** cut faces.

The behavioural delta vs Task 2.4's body: today, a tangent segment still runs the face loop on
its (volume-unchanged) result compound, polluting `mergedTags` with original-face entries derived
from the unchanged geometry. SS-K-10 surfaces this by asserting the cut-face count comes purely
from the valid segment. The fix is the §5.2 "abs(volume(next) - volume(running)) < 1e-6 * volume"
skip.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test — SS-K-10**

```ts
it('SS-K-10: one tangent segment + one valid segment → ok:true, console.warn, only valid segment contributes', async () => {
  const box = makeBox(oc, 100, 60, 40);
  const compound = buildTestCompound(oc, box);

  // Segment 1: plane at z=40 with normal +Z and arrowDirection=1 — the half-space
  // removed is +Z, which sits ABOVE the top face of the box (z<=40). The cut is
  // a no-op: volume unchanged, plane coincident with the top face.
  // Segment 2: plane at x=50 with normal +X — a real vertical jog.
  const planes: Plane[] = [
    { origin: { x: 0,  y: 0, z: 40 }, normal: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 1 } }, // tangent
    { origin: { x: 50, y: 0, z: 0  }, normal: { x: 1, y: 0, z: 0 } }, // valid
  ];

  const warnSpy = vi.spyOn(console, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
  try {
    const result = cutByStepSegments(oc, compound, planes, 1);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) return;

    // Per-segment tangent must trigger a console warning naming the segment index.
    const warnCalls = warnSpy.mock.calls.map(c => String(c[0] ?? ''));
    expect(warnCalls.some(s => /tangent segment 0/.test(s))).toBe(true);

    // Only the valid segment (index 1) contributed cut faces. The kept compound
    // should be the right half of the box removed — i.e. one new cut face at x=50.
    const tags = [...result.faceTags.values()];
    const cutFaces = tags.filter(t => t.tag === 'cut-face');
    expect(cutFaces.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    for (const face of cutFaces) {
      expect((face as { planeSource?: string }).planeSource).toBe('section');
    }
    // Approx volume: 50 * 60 * 40 = 120 000 mm³ (right half removed).
    const vol = shapeVolumePublic(oc, result.compound);
    expect(vol).toBeGreaterThan(115_000);
    expect(vol).toBeLessThan(125_000);

    disposeOcctShape(oc, result.compound);
    (box.handle as { delete(): void }).delete();
  } finally {
    warnSpy.mockRestore();
  }
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement per-segment volume-tangent skip**

Inside `cutByStepSegments`, between the no-solid guard (Task 2.4) and the face-explorer loop,
add the §5.2 volume-tangent check. When triggered, release `next` and `halfSpace`, log a
console.warn naming the segment index, and `continue` the loop without merging this segment's
faces.

Specifically:

```ts
    // Per-segment volume-tangent check (spec §5.2 / §5.4).
    // If the cut didn't change volume, this segment's plane was coincident with
    // an existing face. Per-segment tangent is non-fatal: skip the merge, keep
    // running, and proceed to the next segment.
    const volRunning = shapeVolume(oc, running);
    const volNext    = shapeVolume(oc, next);
    if (volRunning > 0 && Math.abs(volNext - volRunning) < Math.max(volRunning, 1) * 1e-6) {
      console.warn(`cutByStepSegments: tangent segment ${i}, skipping`);
      (next as unknown as { delete?(): void }).delete?.();
      continue; // running stays as-is; mergedTags untouched for this i
    }
```

`shapeVolume` is the existing private helper in the same file (line ~313) — reuse, don't
duplicate. If the function isn't visible to `cutByStepSegments` because of the file order,
either move `cutByStepSegments` below `shapeVolume` or extract `shapeVolume` to a top-level
declaration; both are surgical.

### Task 2.6: `cutAssemblyByStepSegments` + SS-INT-1 / SS-INT-2 / SS-INT-3 + Batch 2 verification

**Spec section:** §5.5 (assembly variant), §4.3 (α-invariant clauses 4/5/6), §10.1 (SS-INT-1..3)
**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/kernel/src/section/cutBySectionPlane.ts` (add `cutAssemblyByStepSegments`)
- Modify: `packages/kernel/src/section/cutBySectionPlane.spec.ts` (append SS-INT-1, SS-INT-2, SS-INT-3 + assembly test)
- Re-run: full kernel suite for Batch 2 exit

> **Naming convention note (id range disambiguation):** Tests SS-INT-1..3 below are
> *integration-level* guards that the kernel batch imports the same `deriveStepPlanes` helper
> that Batch 1 ships. They re-exercise SS-SP-4d / SS-SP-4e / SS-SP-4f (Batch 1's clause-4/5/6
> tests on the shared helper) at the kernel-package boundary to catch wiring errors — e.g. a
> stale import, an accidentally re-implemented validator inside the kernel, or a broken
> re-export across package boundaries. The kernel itself does NOT re-implement clauses 4/5/6 —
> it delegates to `deriveStepPlanes`. An earlier draft of this plan numbered these tests
> inside the kernel SS-K- range, which overlapped with Batch 1's SS-SP-4d/e/f clause checks;
> they were renamed to SS-INT-1..3 to make the integration role explicit. Kernel-internal
> tests retain the SS-K- prefix: SS-K-1..5, SS-K-9, SS-K-10.

   between the command and the kernel — they belong in the kernel spec because they document
   the contract the kernel relies on.

```ts
it('SS-K assembly: cutAssemblyByStepSegments — 2-component fixture, both components cut by both planes', () => {
  // Mirror the existing cutAssemblyBySectionPlane test fixture pattern.
  const asm: import('@zilventor/shared').AssemblyDocument = {
    id: 'ss-asm', name: 'stepped-asm', units: 'mm', parameters: {},
    components: [
      { id: 'c1', partPath: 'p1.zipt', position: { x: 0,   y: 0,   z: 0 }, rotation: { rx: 0, ry: 0, rz: 0 }, visible: true },
      { id: 'c2', partPath: 'p2.zipt', position: { x: 0,   y: 80,  z: 0 }, rotation: { rx: 0, ry: 0, rz: 0 }, visible: true },
    ],
    constraints: [], groundedComponentPaths: [], colors: {}, bomItemNumbers: {},
    cutListItemNumbers: {}, colorOverrides: {}, properties: {}, materials: [], hardware: [],
  };
  const bodyResolver = (_partId: string, pose: { translation: { x: number; y: number; z: number } }) => {
    const mk = new oc.BRepPrimAPI_MakeBox_2(100, 60, 40);
    const raw = mk.Shape(); mk.delete();
    const tx = new oc.gp_Trsf_1();
    const tv = new oc.gp_Vec_4(pose.translation.x, pose.translation.y, pose.translation.z);
    (tx as unknown as { SetTranslation_1(v: unknown): void }).SetTranslation_1(tv as never);
    const xf = new oc.BRepBuilderAPI_Transform_2(raw as never, tx as never, true as never);
    const shape = (xf as unknown as { Shape(): unknown }).Shape();
    (xf as unknown as { delete(): void }).delete();
    (tx as unknown as { delete(): void }).delete();
    (tv as unknown as { delete(): void }).delete();
    return { __brand: 'OcctShape' as const, handle: shape };
  };
  const planes: Plane[] = [
    { origin: { x: 0,  y: 0, z: 20 }, normal: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 1 } },
    { origin: { x: 50, y: 0, z: 0  }, normal: { x: 1, y: 0, z: 0 } },
  ];
  const result = cutAssemblyByStepSegments(oc, asm, planes, 1, {
    lookupSubAsm: () => undefined,
    partPathToId: new Map([['p1.zipt', 'part-1'], ['p2.zipt', 'part-2']]),
    bodyResolver,
    materialResolver: () => 'mat-default',
  });
  expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
  if (!result.ok) return;

  const tags = [...result.faceTags.values()];
  const sectionFaces = tags.filter(t => t.tag === 'cut-face' && (t as { planeSource?: string }).planeSource === 'section');
  // At minimum 2 cut faces per component × 2 components = 4. Lower bound 2 for robustness against OCCT face merging.
  expect(sectionFaces.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
  // No 'split' tags from this code path.
  const splitFaces = tags.filter(t => (t as { planeSource?: string }).planeSource === 'split');
  expect(splitFaces).toHaveLength(0);

  disposeOcctShape(oc, result.compound);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `cutAssemblyByStepSegments`**

The function mirrors `cutAssemblyBySectionPlane` (existing at line ~350) exactly, swapping the
inner per-leaf call from `cutBySectionPlane` to `cutByStepSegments` and dropping the
splitPlane/splitSide params. Append to `packages/kernel/src/section/cutBySectionPlane.ts` after
the existing `cutAssemblyBySectionPlane` function:

```ts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// cutAssemblyByStepSegments (Drawing F1.2 — assembly mirror of cutByStepSegments)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Cut every leaf part in `asm` by the stepped polyline `planes`/`arrowDirection`
 * and compose the per-component results into a single compound + merged
 * FaceTagMap. Mirrors `cutAssemblyBySectionPlane`'s structure exactly; only the
 * per-leaf call differs.
 *
 * Components that produce { ok: false, reason: 'empty' } are silently skipped
 * (consistent with the existing assembly section path). Per-component
 * count-< 2 / count-> 8 throws from `cutByStepSegments` are NOT caught here —
 * those are programmer errors that must surface to the caller.
 */
export function cutAssemblyByStepSegments(
  oc: OcctInstance,
  asm: AssemblyDocument,
  planes: readonly Plane[],
  arrowDirection: -1 | 1,
  opts: AssemblyCutOptions,
): Result {
  const leaves = flattenLeaves(asm, opts.lookupSubAsm, opts.partPathToId);

  const builder = new oc.BRep_Builder();
  const resultCompound = new oc.TopoDS_Compound();
  builder.MakeCompound(resultCompound);

  const mergedTags: FaceTagMap = new Map();
  let anyOk = false;

  for (const { partId, pose } of leaves) {
    const body = opts.bodyResolver(partId, pose);
    if (!body) continue;

    const srcBuilder = new oc.BRep_Builder();
    const srcComp = new oc.TopoDS_Compound();
    srcBuilder.MakeCompound(srcComp);
    srcBuilder.Add(srcComp, body.handle as never);
    srcBuilder.delete();

    const r = cutByStepSegments(oc, srcComp, planes, arrowDirection);
    srcComp.delete();

    if (!r.ok) {
      // 'empty' is non-fatal at the assembly level (matches F1's anyOk pattern).
      // The kernel-module cutByStepSegments only ever returns 'empty' as its
      // failure reason — never 'tangent' — so no console.warn branch is needed.
      continue;
    }

    anyOk = true;
    builder.Add(resultCompound, r.compound as never);
    (r.compound as unknown as { delete?(): void }).delete?.();

    const matId = opts.materialResolver(partId);
    for (const [key, val] of r.faceTags) {
      mergedTags.set(key, {
        tag: val.tag,
        partId,
        materialId: matId,
        ...(val.polygon3D ? { polygon3D: val.polygon3D } : {}),
        ...(val.planeSource ? { planeSource: val.planeSource } : {}),
      });
    }
  }

  builder.delete();

  if (!anyOk) {
    resultCompound.delete();
    return { ok: false, reason: 'empty' };
  }

  return { ok: true, compound: resultCompound, faceTags: mergedTags };
}
```


- [ ] **Step 2: Add `cutByStepSegments` + `cutAssemblyByStepSegments` exports to the app-side wrapper**

Append to `packages/app/src/kernel/cutBySectionPlane.ts`, mirroring the existing

```ts
export async function cutByStepSegments(
  planes: readonly Plane[],
  arrowDirection: -1 | 1,
): Promise<KernelCutResult> {
  const kernel = useAppStore.getState().kernel;
  if (!kernel) throw new Error('kernel-not-ready');
  return await kernel.cutByStepSegments(planes, arrowDirection);
}

export async function cutAssemblyByStepSegments(
  compounds: readonly unknown[],
  planes: readonly Plane[],
  arrowDirection: -1 | 1,
): Promise<KernelAssemblyCutResult> {
  const kernel = useAppStore.getState().kernel;
  if (!kernel) throw new Error('kernel-not-ready');
  return await kernel.cutAssemblyByStepSegments(compounds, planes, arrowDirection);
}
```

## Batch 3: `AddSectionViewCommand` extension + cache key + projector α-guard + fingerprint extension (~6 tasks)

Batch 3 wires the new `'stepped'` mode through the command boundary. The command extends its
payload type with the `'stepped'` literal and the `stepSegments?` field, branches the `apply()` body
on `mode === 'stepped'` so it derives N planes via `deriveStepPlanes` (from Batch 1), invokes the
new `cutByStepSegments` kernel call (from Batch 2), and emits an extended OPFS cache key per spec
§9.2. The α-invariant is enforced at the command layer (§4.3 layer 2) by calling
`validateSteppedInvariant` BEFORE plane derivation, and at the projector layer (§4.3 layer 3) so a
corrupt manifest read also throws cleanly. The fingerprint is extended per spec §9.3 to
incorporate every segment so a vertex move of ≥ 1 µm changes the hash.

No tool-mode work, no UI, no renderer changes — those land in Batch 4. The exit gate is that
programmatic stepped-section creation works end-to-end at the data-model level: the command
applies, the cache hits on the second identical call, the revert evicts the cache, and the
fingerprint encodes the segments. The app package gains ~5 unit tests (SS-CMD-1..SS-CMD-5); the
shared package gains ~2 unit tests (one projector α-guard, one fingerprint regression).

> **Baselines (at Batch 3 start, after Batches 1 + 2):**
> - `@zilventor/app` tests: ~existing baseline (unchanged by Batches 1–2; Batches 1–2 touched
>   `shared` and `kernel` only).
> - `@zilventor/shared` tests: ~515 (503 baseline + ~12 from Batch 1).
> - `@zilventor/kernel` tests: ~existing + 10 from Batch 2 (SS-K-1..SS-K-10).
>
> **Pre-read before implementing (mandatory — see Task 3.1):**
> - `packages/app/src/commands/AddSectionViewCommand.ts` — full file (210 lines). The current
>   `AddSectionViewPayload` is at lines 16–26; `apply()` is at 35–184; `revert()` is at 186–209.
>   `this.cacheKey: string | null` field is at line 31 and is already used by `revert()` to call
>   `sectionCache.delete(this.cacheKey)` at line 195. No revert wiring change is needed —
>   Task 3.5 is a regression test only.
> - `packages/app/src/commands/__tests__/AddSectionViewCommand.test.ts` — **filename is `.test.ts`,
>   NOT `.spec.ts`** (this is the existing convention in this folder; do NOT rename). The file
>   already mocks `cutBySectionPlane`, `cutAssemblyBySectionPlane`, `sectionCache.{get,put,delete}`,
>   `projectSectionView`, and `deriveSectionPlane` + `deriveSplitPlane`. Batch 3 extends those mocks
>   to also cover `cutByStepSegments`, `cutAssemblyByStepSegments`, `deriveStepPlanes`, and
>   `validateSteppedInvariant`.
> - `packages/app/src/kernel/cutBySectionPlane.ts` — Batch 2 adds `cutByStepSegments` and
>   `cutAssemblyByStepSegments` named exports adjacent to the existing `cutBySectionPlane`.
> - `packages/shared/src/drawing/projectSectionView.ts:111–115` — current hatch loop with the
>   `planeSource === 'split'` filter at line 114. Task 3.6 adds an α-invariant guard ABOVE that
>   loop (before any face iteration) so corrupt manifest reads throw `'stepped-section-invariant-violated'`.
> - `packages/shared/src/drawing/fingerprint.ts:131–163` — current `fingerprintSectionView`.
>   Task 3.6 appends a `|stepped|<v0.x>,<v0.y>;...` suffix when `view.mode === 'stepped'`.

```ts
describe('AddSectionViewCommand.apply() — stepped-section happy path (SS-CMD-1)', () => {
  const segments: readonly StepSegment[] = [
    { startSheet: { x: 0,   y: 0 },  endSheet: { x: 60,  y: 0 } },
    { startSheet: { x: 60,  y: 0 },  endSheet: { x: 60,  y: 30 } },
    { startSheet: { x: 60,  y: 30 }, endSheet: { x: 120, y: 30 } },
  ];

  it('mode=stepped with valid 3-segment payload → sheet.views gains one SectionView with mode=stepped + stepSegments', async () => {
    const cmd = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: segments,
    });
    await cmd.apply();

    const doc = useAppStore.getState().activeDocument;
    if (!doc || doc.kind !== 'drawing') throw new Error('no drawing');
    const sheet = doc.document.sheets.find((s) => s.id === 's1')!;
    expect(sheet.views).toHaveLength(2);

    const sv = sheet.views.find((v) => v.kind === 'section') as SectionView & {
      mode?: string;
      stepSegments?: readonly StepSegment[];
      cutSide?: unknown;
      splitPlane?: unknown;
    };
    expect(sv).toBeDefined();
    expect(sv.mode).toBe('stepped');
    expect(sv.stepSegments).toEqual(segments);
    // Per spec §4.4: sectionLine on a stepped view is V0 → V_last (convenience).
    expect(sv.sectionLine.p0).toEqual({ x: 0, y: 0 });
    expect(sv.sectionLine.p1).toEqual({ x: 120, y: 30 });
    // α-invariant clause 7: cutSide and splitPlane MUST be absent.
    expect(sv.cutSide).toBeUndefined();
    expect(sv.splitPlane).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('apply(mode=stepped) routes through cutByStepSegments, NOT cutBySectionPlane', async () => {
    const cmd = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: segments,
    });
    await cmd.apply();
    expect(cutByStepSegments).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(cutBySectionPlane).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('apply(mode=stepped) calls validateSteppedInvariant BEFORE plane derivation (layer 2)', async () => {
    const cmd = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: segments,
    });
    await cmd.apply();
    // The validator must have been called at least once at the command boundary.
    expect(validateSteppedInvariant).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

The `apply()` body does not yet branch on `mode === 'stepped'`, so it falls through the

TypeScript literal `'stepped'`, but the rest of the apply() body still uses the F1 path).
In practice the test will fail at the `expect(cutByStepSegments).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)` line
first because `cutBySectionPlane` is called instead.

3a. Extend the imports at line 6 and add the new kernel import at line 7:

```ts
import { deriveSectionPlane, deriveSplitPlane, deriveStepPlanes, validateSteppedInvariant } from '@zilventor/shared/src/drawing/sectionPlane';
import { cutBySectionPlane, cutAssemblyBySectionPlane, cutByStepSegments, cutAssemblyByStepSegments } from '../kernel/cutBySectionPlane';
```

3b. Just after the `parentView` resolution (after current line 45), insert the stepped-mode
early-return branch. Replace the current `apply()` body from "Build a minimal SectionView for
resolveSectionSource lookup purposes" (line 47) to the closing brace of the method (line 184) with
the following structure. The full / half path is preserved verbatim from the current
implementation; only the stepped branch is new:

```ts
async apply(): Promise<void> {
  const state = useAppStore.getState();
  const doc = state.activeDocument;
  if (!doc || doc.kind !== 'drawing') return;

  const drawing = doc.document;
  const sheet = drawing.sheets.find((s) => s.id === this.payload.sheetId);
  if (!sheet) throw new Error(`sheet ${this.payload.sheetId} not found`);

  const parentView = sheet.views.find((v) => v.id === this.payload.parentViewId);
  if (!parentView) throw new Error('parent view not found');

  // ── F1.2: stepped-section branch ────────────────────────────────────────
  if (this.payload.mode === 'stepped') {
    return await this.applyStepped(state, drawing, sheet, parentView);
  }
  // ── F1 + F1.1: full / half branch (unchanged) ───────────────────────────
  return await this.applyPlanar(state, drawing, sheet, parentView);
}
```

3c. Move the existing apply() body (lines 47–184) into a private method `applyPlanar`. This is a
pure extract — no logic change. Signature:

```ts
private async applyPlanar(
  state: ReturnType<typeof useAppStore.getState>,
  drawing: DrawingDocument,
  sheet: DrawingSheet,
  parentView: DrawingSheet['views'][number],
): Promise<void> {
  // ── existing body (lines 47–184), verbatim — only renamed parameters ──
  // The body references `state`, `drawing`, `sheet`, `parentView` exactly as
  // the original. No further changes.
}
```

(Implementer: if the extract turns out to be invasive — e.g., the function captures many local
variables tied to `this.payload` — keep the body inline in `apply()` and instead guard the
stepped branch with an `if (this.payload.mode === 'stepped') { ...; return; }` block at the top
of `apply()` directly. The structural goal is one branch per mode, not one method per mode.)

3d. Add the new `applyStepped` method:

```ts
private async applyStepped(
  state: ReturnType<typeof useAppStore.getState>,
  drawing: DrawingDocument,
  sheet: DrawingSheet,
  parentView: DrawingSheet['views'][number],
): Promise<void> {
  const stepSegments = this.payload.stepSegments;
  if (!stepSegments || stepSegments.length < 2) {
    throw new Error('stepped-section-invariant-violated: stepSegments must have length ≥ 2');
  }
  // α-invariant clause 7 — stepped + half combo rejected at command boundary.
  if (this.payload.cutSide !== undefined) {
    throw new Error('stepped-section-invariant-violated: cutSide must be undefined when mode==="stepped"');
  }

  // Layer 2 of α-invariant (helper layer = layer 1; projector = layer 3 — Task 3.6).
  // The validator runs BEFORE any plane derivation so a programmatic misuse is caught early.
  // It accepts the partial-view shape we are about to construct.
  validateSteppedInvariant({
    mode: 'stepped',
    stepSegments,
    cutSide: undefined,
    splitPlane: undefined,
  } as unknown as Parameters<typeof validateSteppedInvariant>[0]);

  // Convenience sectionLine: V0 → V_last (per spec §4.4). Used by the overlay's existing
  // arrowhead + label logic. NOT authoritative geometry — stepSegments is.
  const p0 = stepSegments[0]!.startSheet;
  const p1 = stepSegments[stepSegments.length - 1]!.endSheet;

  // resolveSectionSource lookup needs a SectionView shape. Use a placeholder.
  const source = resolveSectionSource(
    {
      id: '__pending__',
      kind: 'section',
      parentViewId: this.payload.parentViewId,
      sectionLine: { p0, p1 },
      arrowDirection: this.payload.arrowDirection,
      label: '',
      positionMm: this.payload.dropAt,
      scale: parentView.scale,
      showHidden: false,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments,
      hatchSegments: [],
      fingerprint: '',
    } satisfies SectionView,
    drawing,
  );

  const viewMatrix = ((parentView as { viewMatrix?: number[] }).viewMatrix
    ?? (new Array(16).fill(0) as number[])) as import('@zilventor/shared/src/drawing/viewMatrix').Mat4;

  const planes = deriveStepPlanes(stepSegments, viewMatrix, this.payload.arrowDirection);

  // Cache key per spec §9.2:
  //   key = `${sourceId}::${planeFp_0}::${planeFp_1}::...::${planeFp_(N-1)}::stepped`
  const planeFps = planes.map((p) => fingerprintPlane(p)).join('::');
  const key = `${source.id}::${planeFps}::stepped`;
  this.cacheKey = key;

  let compound: unknown;
  let faceTags: FaceTagMap;
  const cached = await sectionCache.get(key);
  if (cached) {
    compound = cached.brep;
    faceTags = cached.faceTags;
  } else {
    const kernelId = source.kind === 'assembly'
      ? (state.activeAssembly?.id ?? source.id)
      : (state.activePart?.id ?? source.id);
    const result = source.kind === 'assembly'
      ? await cutAssemblyByStepSegments(kernelId, planes, this.payload.arrowDirection)
      : await cutByStepSegments(kernelId, planes, this.payload.arrowDirection);

    if (!result.ok) {
      throw new Error('stepped-section-empty-cut');
    }
    compound = result.compound;
    faceTags = result.faceTags;
    await sectionCache.put(key, compound, faceTags);
  }

  const materials =
    state.activePart?.materials ??
    state.activeAssembly?.materials ??
    [];
  const hatchLookup = (materialId: string): { angleDeg: number; spacingMm: number } => {
    const mat = materials.find((m) => m.id === materialId);
    return getHatchSpec(mat ?? { id: materialId, name: '', defaultThickness: 0, color: '#808080' });
  };

  // For stepped views the projector still wants ONE sectionViewMatrix. Re-use the FIRST plane's
  // matrix as the canonical projection frame; the kernel has already merged all face tags into a
  // single FaceTagMap so projector hatch generation works uniformly.
  const { sectionViewMatrix } = deriveSectionPlane(
    { p0, p1 },
    viewMatrix,
    parentView.scale.numerator / parentView.scale.denominator,
    this.payload.arrowDirection,
  );

  const scale = parentView.scale.numerator / parentView.scale.denominator;
  const { edges, hatchSegments, fingerprint } = projectSectionView(
    compound as Parameters<typeof projectSectionView>[0],
    faceTags,
    scale,
    hatchLookup,
    sectionViewMatrix,
  );

  const existingLabels = sheet.views
    .filter((v): v is SectionView | DetailView =>
      v.kind === 'section' || v.kind === 'detail')
    .map((v) => v.label);
  const label = allocateCalloutLabel(existingLabels);

  const newId = crypto.randomUUID();
  this.newViewId = newId;

  const sectionView: SectionView = {
    id: newId,
    kind: 'section',
    parentViewId: this.payload.parentViewId,
    sectionLine: { p0, p1 },
    arrowDirection: this.payload.arrowDirection,
    label,
    positionMm: this.payload.dropAt,
    scale: parentView.scale,
    showHidden: false,
    mode: 'stepped',
    stepSegments,
    hatchSegments,
    fingerprint,
    // NB: NO `plane` — stepped views have N planes; the legacy `plane?` field is omitted.
    // NB: NO `cutSide` and NO `splitPlane` (α-invariant clause 7).
    projection: {
      visibleEdges: edges,
      hiddenEdges: [],
      bbox: { minX: 0, minY: 0, maxX: 0, maxY: 0 },
    },
    ...(sectionViewMatrix ? { inputFingerprint: Array.from(sectionViewMatrix).slice(0, 4).join(',') } : {}),
  };

  const nextDoc: DrawingDocument = {
    ...drawing,
    sheets: drawing.sheets.map((s) =>
      s.id === this.payload.sheetId
        ? { ...s, views: [...s.views, sectionView] }
        : s,
    ),
  };
  useAppStore.setState({ activeDocument: { kind: 'drawing', document: nextDoc }, activeDrawing: nextDoc });
}
```

include it:

```ts
describe('AddSectionViewCommand.apply() — stepped α-invariant guards (SS-CMD-2 + SS-CMD-3)', () => {
  it('SS-CMD-2: mode="stepped" with stepSegments=undefined throws stepped-section-invariant-violated', async () => {
    const cmd = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      // intentionally omit stepSegments
    });
    await expect(cmd.apply()).rejects.toThrow(/stepped-section-invariant-violated/);
    // The guard fires BEFORE kernel dispatch: no cut call should have been issued.
    expect(cutByStepSegments).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(cutBySectionPlane).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('SS-CMD-2b: mode="stepped" with stepSegments.length === 1 throws (clause 2: length ≥ 2)', async () => {
    const oneSeg: readonly StepSegment[] = [
      { startSheet: { x: 0, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 60, y: 0 } },
    ];
    const cmd = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: oneSeg,
    });
    await expect(cmd.apply()).rejects.toThrow(/stepped-section-invariant-violated/);
    expect(cutByStepSegments).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('SS-CMD-3: mode="stepped" with cutSide set throws (clause 7: stepped + half rejected)', async () => {
    const segments: readonly StepSegment[] = [
      { startSheet: { x: 0,  y: 0 },  endSheet: { x: 60, y: 0 } },
      { startSheet: { x: 60, y: 0 },  endSheet: { x: 60, y: 30 } },
    ];
    const cmd = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: segments,
      cutSide: 1,
    });
    await expect(cmd.apply()).rejects.toThrow(/stepped-section-invariant-violated/);
    // Crucially, the guard fires BEFORE plane derivation and kernel cut.
    expect(cutByStepSegments).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('SS-CMD-3b: when SS-CMD-3 throws, no SectionView is appended to sheet.views', async () => {
    const before = useAppStore.getState().activeDocument;
    const sheetBefore = before && before.kind === 'drawing'
      ? before.document.sheets.find((s) => s.id === 's1')!
      : null;
    const initialViewCount = sheetBefore ? sheetBefore.views.length : 0;

    const segments: readonly StepSegment[] = [
      { startSheet: { x: 0,  y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 60, y: 0 } },
      { startSheet: { x: 60, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 60, y: 30 } },
    ];
    const cmd = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: segments,
      cutSide: -1,
    });
    await expect(cmd.apply()).rejects.toThrow();

    const after = useAppStore.getState().activeDocument;
    const sheetAfter = after && after.kind === 'drawing'
      ? after.document.sheets.find((s) => s.id === 's1')!
      : null;
    expect(sheetAfter?.views.length).toBe(initialViewCount);
  });
});
```

### Task 3.4: Cache key extension + SS-CMD-5 cache-hit regression

**Spec section:** §9.2

**Files:**
- Read-only: `packages/app/src/commands/AddSectionViewCommand.ts` (verify Task 3.2's cache-key construction matches the spec template verbatim).
- Modify: `packages/app/src/commands/__tests__/AddSectionViewCommand.test.ts` (add SS-CMD-5).

**Red→green→refactor slice owned by this task:** Two identical `apply(mode='stepped', stepSegments=S)`
calls on the same source produce the same cache key, and the **second** call hits the cache so
`cutByStepSegments` is NOT re-invoked. Cache-key format follows spec §9.2 exactly:

```
key = `${sourceId}::${planeFp_0}::${planeFp_1}::...::${planeFp_(N-1)}::stepped`
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Append to `packages/app/src/commands/__tests__/AddSectionViewCommand.test.ts`:

```ts
describe('AddSectionViewCommand.apply() — stepped cache key (SS-CMD-5)', () => {
  const segments: readonly StepSegment[] = [
    { startSheet: { x: 0,   y: 0 },  endSheet: { x: 60,  y: 0 } },
    { startSheet: { x: 60,  y: 0 },  endSheet: { x: 60,  y: 30 } },
    { startSheet: { x: 60,  y: 30 }, endSheet: { x: 120, y: 30 } },
  ];

  it('SS-CMD-5a: cache key matches spec §9.2 template — ends with "::stepped"', async () => {
    const cmd = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: segments,
    });
    await cmd.apply();
    const putCall = (sectionCache.put as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls[0];
    const cacheKey = putCall?.[0] as string;
    expect(cacheKey).toBeDefined();
    expect(cacheKey.endsWith('::stepped')).toBe(true);
    // The key must contain N=3 plane fingerprints (deriveStepPlanes mock returns 3 planes).
    // Each fingerprint is separated by '::'. Header is `${sourceId}`, trailer is `::stepped`.
    // So the count of '::' is N + 1 (sourceId, fp0, fp1, fp2, stepped → 4 separators).
    const separatorCount = (cacheKey.match(/::/g) ?? []).length;
    expect(separatorCount).toBe(4);
  });

  it('SS-CMD-5b: stepped cache key differs from full-section key on the same parent', async () => {
    const fullCmd = new AddSectionViewCommand({ ...basePayload, mode: 'full' });
    await fullCmd.apply();
    const fullKey = (sectionCache.put as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls[0]?.[0] as string;

    vi.clearAllMocks();
    (sectionCache.get as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mockResolvedValue(null);
    (cutBySectionPlane as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, compound: {}, faceTags: new Map() });
    (cutByStepSegments as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, compound: {}, faceTags: new Map() });

    const steppedCmd = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: segments,
    });
    await steppedCmd.apply();
    const steppedKey = (sectionCache.put as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls[0]?.[0] as string;

    expect(fullKey).toBeDefined();
    expect(steppedKey).toBeDefined();
    expect(steppedKey).not.toBe(fullKey);
  });

  it('SS-CMD-5c: two identical stepped applies → second uses cache hit; cutByStepSegments NOT re-invoked', async () => {
    // First apply: cache miss, cut runs.
    const cmd1 = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: segments,
    });
    await cmd1.apply();
    expect(cutByStepSegments).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const cacheKey = (sectionCache.put as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls[0]?.[0] as string;
    expect(cacheKey).toBeDefined();

    // Reset call counters but seed cache.get to RETURN a cached entry for this key.
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    (sectionCache.get as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mockImplementation(async (k: string) =>
      k === cacheKey ? { brep: {}, faceTags: new Map() } : null,
    );

    // Second apply with identical payload: cache hit, cut MUST NOT be re-invoked.
    const cmd2 = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: segments,
    });
    await cmd2.apply();
    expect(cutByStepSegments).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(sectionCache.put).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```


**Expected outcome:** If Task 3.2 already built the cache key exactly per spec §9.2 (with the
`::stepped` trailer), SS-CMD-5a and SS-CMD-5b pass first try; SS-CMD-5c may pass first try too
because the cache-hit branch is the existing F1 pattern reused verbatim.

If the cache key omits the `::stepped` trailer (e.g. an earlier draft used `::N=3` or no
discriminator), SS-CMD-5a fails with:

```
AssertionError: expected '<sourceId>::<fp0>::<fp1>::<fp2>' to end with '::stepped'
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement (only if Step 2 failed)**

Verify and tighten the cache-key construction inside `applyStepped` to match spec §9.2 verbatim:

```ts
// Per spec §9.2:
//   key = `${sourceId}::${planeFp_0}::${planeFp_1}::...::${planeFp_(N-1)}::stepped`
const planeFps = planes.map((p) => fingerprintPlane(p)).join('::');
const key = `${source.id}::${planeFps}::stepped`;
this.cacheKey = key;
```

The trailing `::stepped` discriminator is what prevents collision with the F1 `::${fingerprintPlane(plane)}`
key (which omits any mode suffix) and the F1.1 `::${splitPlaneFp}::${cutSide}` key (which ends in
a numeric `1` / `-1`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```ts
describe('AddSectionViewCommand.revert() — stepped cache eviction (SS-CMD-4)', () => {
  const segments: readonly StepSegment[] = [
    { startSheet: { x: 0,   y: 0 },  endSheet: { x: 60,  y: 0 } },
    { startSheet: { x: 60,  y: 0 },  endSheet: { x: 60,  y: 30 } },
    { startSheet: { x: 60,  y: 30 }, endSheet: { x: 120, y: 30 } },
  ];

  it('SS-CMD-4a: apply stepped → revert → sectionCache.delete called once with the stepped cacheKey', async () => {
    const cmd = new AddSectionViewCommand({
      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: segments,
    });
    await cmd.apply();

    const putCall = (sectionCache.put as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls[0];
    const steppedKey = putCall?.[0] as string;
    expect(steppedKey).toBeDefined();
    expect(steppedKey.endsWith('::stepped')).toBe(true);

    vi.clearAllMocks();
    await cmd.revert();

    expect(sectionCache.delete).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(sectionCache.delete).toHaveBeenCalledWith(steppedKey);

      ...basePayload,
      mode: 'stepped',
      stepSegments: segments,
    });
    await cmd.apply();
    await cmd.revert();
    expect(useAppStore.getState().activeDocument).toEqual(snapshot);
  });
});
```

**Red→green→refactor slice owned by this task:** Two independent regressions, both in
`packages/shared`:

1. `projectSectionView` throws `'stepped-section-invariant-violated'` when handed a `SectionView`
   with `mode: 'stepped'` whose `stepSegments` violates any of the 7 α-invariant clauses
   (simulating a corrupt manifest read).
2. `fingerprintSectionView` produces **different** djb2 hashes for two stepped views that differ
   only in a single segment vertex by ≥ 1 µm; produces **identical** hashes when the vertex
   difference is < 1 nm (i.e., below the 1e-3 mm rounding floor in §9.3).

The two regressions are bundled into one task because they share the same code area (shared
drawing helpers), they share the same Batch 1 dependency (`validateSteppedInvariant`), and
neither alone is large enough to warrant its own batch slot.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { projectSectionView } from '../projectSectionView';

describe('projectSectionView — stepped α-invariant (layer 3) (F1.2)', () => {
  const emptyInput: ProjectorInput = {
    triangles: [] as unknown as ProjectorInput['triangles'],
    edges: [] as unknown as ProjectorInput['edges'],
    bbox: [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0],
  } as unknown as ProjectorInput;
  const emptyFTM: FaceTagMap = new Map();
  const noHatch = (_id: string) => ({ angleDeg: 45, spacingMm: 5 });

  // The projector signature is projectSectionView(input, faceTags, scale, getHatch, matrix).
  // For F1.2 the projector also receives the SectionView shape for the α-invariant guard —
  // implementer should verify the actual signature before writing this test. If the current
  // signature does NOT include the view, this task adds a new optional `view` parameter at
  // the end (last positional or via an options bag).

  it('SS-PR-1: throws when SectionView has mode="stepped" but stepSegments=undefined', () => {
    const view = {
      mode: 'stepped' as const,
      // intentionally no stepSegments — simulates a corrupt manifest read
    } as unknown as SectionView;
    expect(() => projectSectionView(emptyInput, emptyFTM, 1, noHatch, undefined, view))
      .toThrow(/stepped-section-invariant-violated/);
  });

  it('SS-PR-2: throws when stepped view has only 1 segment (clause 2)', () => {
    const view = {
      mode: 'stepped' as const,
      stepSegments: [
        { startSheet: { x: 0, y: 0 }, endSheet: { x: 60, y: 0 } },
      ],
    } as unknown as SectionView;
    expect(() => projectSectionView(emptyInput, emptyFTM, 1, noHatch, undefined, view))
      .toThrow(/stepped-section-invariant-violated/);
  });

  it('SS-PR-3: does NOT throw for a valid stepped view (regression)', () => {
    const view = {
      mode: 'stepped' as const,
      stepSegments: [

      ],

    expect(() => projectSectionView(emptyInput, emptyFTM, 1, noHatch, undefined, view))
      .not.toThrow();

    expect(() => projectSectionView(emptyInput, emptyFTM, 1, noHatch))
      .not.toThrow();

});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { fingerprintSectionView } from '../fingerprint';
import type { SectionView } from '@zilventor/shared';

const baseStepped = (): SectionView => ({
  id: 'sv-1',
  kind: 'section',
  parentViewId: 'bv-front',
  sectionLine: { p0: { x: 0, y: 0 }, p1: { x: 120, y: 30 } },
  arrowDirection: 1,
  label: 'A',
  positionMm: { x: 80, y: 180 },
  scale: { numerator: 1, denominator: 1 },
  showHidden: false,
  mode: 'stepped',
  stepSegments: [
    { startSheet: { x: 0,   y: 0 },  endSheet: { x: 60,  y: 0 } },
    { startSheet: { x: 60,  y: 0 },  endSheet: { x: 60,  y: 30 } },
    { startSheet: { x: 60,  y: 30 }, endSheet: { x: 120, y: 30 } },
  ],
  hatchSegments: [],
  fingerprint: '',
}) as unknown as SectionView;

describe('fingerprintSectionView — stepped extension (F1.2 §9.3)', () => {
  it('SS-FP-1: identical stepped views produce identical fingerprints', () => {
    const a = fingerprintSectionView(baseStepped(), 'parent-fp-1');
    const b = fingerprintSectionView(baseStepped(), 'parent-fp-1');
    expect(a).toBe(b);
  });

  it('SS-FP-2: moving a vertex by ≥ 1 µm produces a different fingerprint', () => {
    const view1 = baseStepped();
    const view2 = baseStepped();
    // Mutate the middle vertex by 1 µm (0.001 mm — exactly the §9.3 rounding floor).
    const seg = { ...view2.stepSegments![1]! };
    seg.endSheet = { x: seg.endSheet.x + 0.001, y: seg.endSheet.y };
    view2.stepSegments = [view2.stepSegments![0]!, seg, view2.stepSegments![2]!] as never;

    const fpA = fingerprintSectionView(view1, 'parent-fp-1');
    const fpB = fingerprintSectionView(view2, 'parent-fp-1');
    expect(fpA).not.toBe(fpB);
  });

  it('SS-FP-3: stepped fingerprint differs from full-section fingerprint with same sectionLine endpoints', () => {
    const stepped = baseStepped();
    const fullEquivalent: SectionView = {
      ...stepped,
      mode: 'full',
      stepSegments: undefined,
    } as unknown as SectionView;
    const fpStepped = fingerprintSectionView(stepped, 'parent-fp-1');
    const fpFull = fingerprintSectionView(fullEquivalent, 'parent-fp-1');
    expect(fpStepped).not.toBe(fpFull);
  });
});
```
